# TASK-25-25: Security Audit for Authentication System

## STATUS
`To Prioritize`

## PRIORITY
`High`

## RISK
`High`

## Related
- Specification: [SPEC-025](../SPECIFICATIONS/SPEC-025.md)
- Feature: [FEAT-025](../FEATURES/FEAT-025.md)

---

## Description
Comprehensive security audit of the authentication system by SecurityAuditor agent. This is MANDATORY for High-risk authentication features.

---

## Acceptance Criteria
- [ ] SQL Injection testing
  - All inputs use parameterized queries
  - No raw SQL with user input
- [ ] XSS prevention testing
  - Input sanitization
  - Output escaping
  - CSP headers configured
- [ ] CSRF protection verification
  - Sanctum CSRF tokens working
  - State parameter in OAuth
- [ ] IDOR protection verification
  - User isolation on all resources
  - Analysis history ownership checks
  - Dashboard data scoping
- [ ] Brute force protection
  - Rate limiting on login
  - Account lockout after failures
  - CAPTCHA consideration
- [ ] Session security
  - Secure cookie settings
  - Token expiration
  - Session regeneration on login
  - Token revocation on logout
- [ ] OAuth security
  - Token encryption at rest
  - Scope minimization
  - Redirect URI validation
  - State parameter validation
- [ ] Password security
  - Strong hashing (bcrypt)
  - Password strength requirements
  - No password in logs/responses
- [ ] Security report generated
- [ ] All findings addressed before deployment

---

## Dependencies
- All implementation tasks (25-1 through 25-24)

---

## Effort Estimate
`Medium` (3-4 hours)

---

## Technical Notes
Security checklist items to verify:
- [ ] HTTPS enforced
- [ ] HSTS header
- [ ] X-Frame-Options header
- [ ] X-Content-Type-Options header
- [ ] Secure cookie flags
- [ ] HttpOnly cookie flags
- [ ] SameSite cookie attribute
- [ ] Token expiration configured
- [ ] Failed login logging (without password)
- [ ] Audit trail for sensitive actions

---

### [2026-03-01 16:30 GMT+2] Agent: Developer
**Security Test Run Results**

Ran the 54 security tests against current backend state:

**Results Summary:**
| Status | Count |
|--------|-------|
| Ã¢Å“â€¦ Passed | 13 |
| Ã¢ÂÂ­Ã¯Â¸Â Skipped | 4 |
| Ã¢Å¡Â Ã¯Â¸Â Risky | 7 |
| Ã¢ÂÅ’ Failed | 30 |

**Passed Tests (13):**
- HttpOnly cookie flag
- SameSite cookie attribute
- OAuth tokens encrypted at rest
- OAuth redirect URI validated
- OAuth token expiration tracked
- OAuth account linking email match
- Password hashed with bcrypt
- Password not in logs
- Password reset token expires
- Password reset token single use
- Failed login logged
- Successful login logged
- Password never logged

**Skipped Tests (4):**
- OAuth state validated (Google OAuth not configured)
- OAuth state entropy (Google OAuth not configured)
- OAuth scope minimization (Google OAuth not configured)
- HSTS header (production only)

**Key Failures Analysis:**

1. **Routes Not Implemented (404 errors):**
   - `/api/v1/auth/login` - login endpoint
   - `/api/v1/auth/register` - registration endpoint
   - `/api/v1/auth/logout` - logout endpoint
   - `/api/v1/auth/forgot-password` - password reset
   - `/api/v1/auth/user` - user profile
   - `/sanctum/csrf-cookie` - CSRF endpoint
   - `/api/v1/analyses/*` - analysis endpoints
   - `/api/v1/dashboard` - dashboard endpoint

2. **Sanctum Not Installed:**
   - Error: `Class "Laravel\Sanctum\Sanctum" not found`
   - Affects: IDOR tests, profile update tests, session tests

3. **Security Headers Missing:**
   - `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` not set
   - `X-Frame-Options: DENY/SAMEORIGIN` not set
   - CSP header not configured

4. **Configuration Issues:**
   - `sanctum.expiration` is null (no token expiration)
   - Rate limiting not configured on auth endpoints
